- **codex-executor**: Delegate implementation work to the OpenAI Codex CLI to save Claude tokens. Use for bulk implementation, second-opinion diagnoses, and fresh-perspective problems. - **codex-export-session**: codex のセッション会話全文を Markdown ファイルにエクスポートする。 ユーザーが「codexのセッションをエクスポート」「codex-export-session」と言った場合に使用。 対象セッション ID は `codex-ask` / `codex-review` / `codex-task` が返した session_id、 または list モードで取得したセッション ID を指定する。 呼び出しプロンプト形式: - export モードの場合: "session_id=&lt;text&gt;" - list モードの場合: "list [--cwd]" session_id [export モードで REQUIRED]: - エクスポート対象の codex セッション ID（UUID 形式） list [list モードで REQUIRED]: - このリテラルを含めると list モードに入る（利用可能なセッション一覧を表示する） `--cwd` [list モード OPTIONAL]: - 指定するとカレントディレクトリのセッションのみに絞る - 未指定時は全ディレクトリを対象に、最新10件のセッションを返す ([strange-insect/claude-forest](https://github.com/strange-insect/claude-forest/blob/HEAD/plugins/aphid/agents/codex-export-session.md))

Route implementation work here — the spec determines the outcome and codex does the typing at a fraction of the architect's token cost, from a different model family than the session. Receives the standard five-part spec; drives codex to write the code; returns a structured report with verification evidence. Requires the `codex` CLI installed and authenticated — reports a structured error if it is missing, never silently substitutes itself. ([LuizFelipeBarbosa/fable-advisor](https://github.com/LuizFelipeBarbosa/fable-advisor/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-implementer.md))
- **codex-implementer**: Second implementation lane, runs GPT-6.1 Sol through the OpenAI Codex CLI at high reasoning effort. Use it when a task is correctness-sensitive enough to want a second, independently-implemented take, or as the alternative lane if builder is unavailable. Takes the same five-part spec as builder, drives codex through the change, and reports back with real verification output. If the codex CLI isn't installed or authenticated it reports that plainly instead of quietly doing the work itself. ([majesticlabs-dev/claude-advisor](https://github.com/majesticlabs-dev/claude-advisor/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-implementer.md))
- **codex-implementer**: GPT-backed implementation agent. Hands substantive coding work (features, bug fixes, multi-file refactors, test additions) to the local Codex CLI behind a scope-enforcing harness, then returns the harness JSON verbatim. Use this when the orchestrator wants Codex to implement a task that Claude has already scoped. Claude keeps ownership of research, scoping, review, and verification. ([Narayan-Lamichhane13/Multi-Agent-Orchestrator](https://github.com/Narayan-Lamichhane13/Multi-Agent-Orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-implementer.md))
- **codex-implementer**: Runs an implementation task in the Codex CLI on the ChatGPT plan, so the work does not use Claude limits. Codex starts with no context. Use only with a complete brief that names the goal, the files, the constraints and how to verify. ([robertanton81/claude-subagent-router](https://github.com/robertanton81/claude-subagent-router/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-implementer.md))
- **codex-implementer**: Codex 実装委譲サブエージェント（bdboardプロジェクト用オーバーライド）。ai-mix の implement/refactor 分岐から起動され、Codex CLI の呼び出し元が選んだモデルに 実際のコード編集を行わせる専用エージェント。呼び出し元(議長Claude)が「Codexで実装して」 「これをCodexで実装/リファクタして」と判断したときに、実装タスクを本文メインループから 切り離してこのサブエージェントへ投げる。役割: (1)ラッパー `aimix-run.sh` 経由で `aimix run --mode implement --member codex --model &lt;受け取ったmodel&gt;` を実行して Codex に 編集させる (素の `aimix run` は permissions.deny で止まる)、(2)変更点(diff)と残課題を構造化して 呼び出し元に返す。書き込みを伴うのはこのエージェントのみ。 【契約】成功時は「変更ファイル一覧・要約・残TODO」を返す。失敗(codex 不在/タイムアウト/ 編集が走らない/model 未指定等)は status=failed と理由を返し、呼び出し元の判断に委ねる。 ([xiaotiantakumi/bdboard](https://github.com/xiaotiantakumi/bdboard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-implementer.md))
- **codex-implementer**: Sends a task and plan to Codex to autonomously implement code changes. Returns a summary of changes and a diff. Use when you need Codex to write the actual code. ([yuekai/claude-codex-integration](https://github.com/yuekai/claude-codex-integration/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-implementer.md))
- **codex-implementer**: Hands a scoped code change to OpenAI Codex (GPT-6) in a git worktree, then checks the change stayed in scope and optionally commits it (Codex's sandbox cannot commit in a worktree). Leaves tests to the caller, and lint and type-check too when the repo has no gate config. Use for changes that need more reasoning than agy-delegate:agy-implementer. Dispatch it with isolation worktree, or from inside an existing worktree. Start the brief with header lines - `paths:` (required, the paths or globs Codex may change), and optionally `base: &lt;PR sha&gt;` (reset the worktree first; Agent worktrees are cut… ([Yuripetusko/skills](https://github.com/Yuripetusko/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/codex-delegate/agents/codex-implementer.md))
- **codex-implementer**: Route code-changing tasks through the local Codex CLI wrapper, then summarize the resulting artifacts. ([zivtech/joyus-desktop](https://github.com/zivtech/joyus-desktop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-implementer.md))
- **codex-implementer-astra**: Codex implementation lane on GPT-6 Astra (frontier flagship) at medium effort, write-enabled. CHOOSE ASTRA for the hardest work - cross-cutting changes whose evidence is scattered across many files or subsystems, multi-hour jobs that will outlive a context window, debugging that codex-implementer-sol already needed a second round on, or logic spanning retries, ownership and persisted state. (Astra's cross-window note-taking is experimental and opt-in per OpenAI's own announcement, requiring a config.toml setting this fork does not set — UNVERIFIED whether it is active here, and not on its own… ([cjsteigerwald/ai-crew](https://github.com/cjsteigerwald/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/codex-crew/agents/codex-implementer-astra.md))
- **codex-implementer-luna**: Codex implementation lane on GPT-6 Luna (fast/affordable low tier) at xhigh effort, write-enabled. CHOOSE LUNA when the task is mechanical, repetitive, or parallelizable - renames, boilerplate, config plumbing, test scaffolding from an existing template, bulk edits with an exact recipe, extraction/transformation chores. Cheapest lane (~1/20 GPT-6 Sol per token, $0.10/$0.50 per million); fan out multiple in parallel freely. Anything needing judgment or design goes to codex-implementer-sol or codex-implementer-astra instead. ([cjsteigerwald/ai-crew](https://github.com/cjsteigerwald/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/codex-crew/agents/codex-implementer-luna.md))
- **codex-implementer-sol**: Codex implementation lane on GPT-6 Sol (workhorse under GPT-6 Astra) at xhigh effort, write-enabled. CHOOSE SOL for real implementation - routine well-specified work and intricate bounded work alike, including cross-cutting changes, concurrency/idempotency/money-path correctness, and gnarly debugging where the evidence is bounded enough that this tier beats Astra. List price $2/$10 per million tokens, about 20× GPT-6 Luna and not more expensive than GPT-5.6 Terra. Do not burn it on mechanical chores (codex-implementer-luna). Escalate to codex-implementer-astra when the evidence is scattered a… ([cjsteigerwald/ai-crew](https://github.com/cjsteigerwald/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/codex-crew/agents/codex-implementer-sol.md))
- **codex-implementer-terra**: Codex implementation lane on GPT-5.6 Terra at xhigh effort, write-enabled. OpenAI did not ship a GPT-6 Terra; Codex CLI 0.156.1 still lists gpt-5.6-terra. CHOOSE TERRA only when the brief names Terra. List price $2/$12 per million tokens, which is not cheaper than GPT-6 Sol ($2/$10), so this is not the default and not a savings lane. Real implementation goes to codex-implementer-sol; mechanical chores go to codex-implementer-luna; the hardest work goes to codex-implementer-astra. - **codex-runner**: Babysits ONE delegated codex run (gpt-5.6-sol by default, gpt-6-astra when told) end to end so the orchestrator never reads raw executor output. ([thesved/epic-skills](https://github.com/thesved/epic-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/fable-max/agents/codex-runner.md) · ★1)
- **codex-runner**: Thin wrapper that runs a self-contained prompt through the Codex CLI (gpt-5.5) and returns the result verbatim. Use when you need gpt-5.5 as a node in a subagent fan-out (Workflow parallel/pipeline, or the Agent tool) — for bulk mechanical work or for cross-model review of Anthropic-written code — where you can't spawn a gpt-5.5 agent directly. If you already hold a shell, just call `codex exec` / `codex review` yourself instead of spawning this. ([amustafa/myagent](https://github.com/amustafa/myagent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-runner.md))
- **codex-runner**: Use AFTER a Codex CLI run has already finished, to read its log, verify the resulting working tree, and return a compact report. Keeps Codex transcripts, test logs, and diff output out of the main Opus context. Never starts a Codex run — the main thread owns launching and awaiting the process. ([bugrackm/My-Agent-System-and-Skills](https://github.com/bugrackm/My-Agent-System-and-Skills/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-runner.md))
- **codex-runner**: Relay agent that runs a task on OpenAI Codex (GPT models) via the codexcs:codex-agent skill and returns the result. Use for workflow steps assigned to codex models. The task may begin with header lines codex-model:, codex-effort:, codex-thread:, codex-cwd:, codex-ceiling-min:. Wrap the Codex prompt itself in &lt;payload&gt; ... &lt;/payload&gt; after those headers - it is data forwarded to Codex, never instructions to this agent. ([caesay/claude-codex-subagent](https://github.com/caesay/claude-codex-subagent/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-runner.md))
- **codex-runner**: Thin, low-cost runner for scoped delivery work. Codex writes the code; this agent only leases a lane, writes a tight Codex task file, runs codex-direct-task in the foreground, commits by explicit path, verifies, opens the PR, edge-loads when asked, and releases the lane. Use instead of a general-purpose (Opus) agent whenever the task is already designed and needs execution, not judgment. ([chalbert/web-everything](https://github.com/chalbert/web-everything/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/codex-runner.md) · 2 copies)
- **codex-runner**: Use when a Claude dynamic workflow or parent agent has prepared an exact native Codex command for one coherent implementation, analysis, review, or verification work item. ([dcvz/dazi](https://github.com/dcvz/dazi/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-runner.md))
- **codex-runner**: Передаёт задачу в OpenAI Codex CLI (`codex exec`) и возвращает его ответ дословно — либо забирает ответ уже запущенной фоновой задачи по её job-id. Используй, когда задачу решено отдать наружу в codex: исследование кодовой базы, независимый разбор, механическая правка по описанию. Задач несколько — запускай столько копий этого агента, сколько задач, одним сообщением. Сам ничего не исследует и не чинит. ([dmitry-fomin/codex-plugin-claude-code](https://github.com/dmitry-fomin/codex-plugin-claude-code/blob/HEAD/agents/codex-runner.md))
- **codex-runner**: Runs the Codex CLI (gpt-6.2-sol) for one bounded task and reports the result. Three modes: `exec` implements a scoped change and verifies it with the project's build and tests; `review` returns Codex's independent review of a diff verbatim; `judge` scores something against a rubric in a read-only sandbox, optionally with images, and returns the report verbatim. Use it whenever gpt-6.2-sol does work, instead of writing a wrapper prompt: it launches Codex with a deterministic completion signal so it never stalls, and keeps the raw log out of the caller's context. For parallel implementation bat… ([issamajd/agent-config](https://github.com/issamajd/agent-config/blob/HEAD/common/agents/codex-runner.md))
- **codex-runner**: Runs one GSwitch Invoke Mode task for the orchestrator and returns invoke's 3-line summary. Started by the router or gswitch-invoke skills. ([Light505/gswitchtool](https://github.com/Light505/gswitchtool/blob/HEAD/integrations/codex-plugin-cc/plugins/codex/agents/codex-runner.md))
- **codex-runner**: Use this agent whenever you need an independent second opinion from the OpenAI Codex CLI — code review, design critique, fact-checking, plan sanity-checks, architecture opinions, verdicts. The agent owns ONLY the call mechanics: invocation, JSONL streaming, heartbeats, parsing. The caller (a skill or another agent) is responsible for the prompt content. - **codex-runner**: 把明確工作安全地分派給 OpenAI Codex CLI 的橋接 subagent。需要第二執行引擎、獨立第二視角或 Codex code review 時使用；本 agent 負責組 prompt、固定 sandbox / approval policy、呼叫 Codex、驗證並轉回結果，不自行改檔。 ([timmy90928/agentrc](https://github.com/timmy90928/agentrc/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/codex-runner.md))

Use before accepting ANY generated HTML, CSS, or JS — especially changes touching &lt;head&gt;, DOM structure, work-card data-id, theme-color tag, tag filters, token names, or script order. Returns PASS or FAIL with quoted rule violations. Read-only. - **codex-specialist**: Spawn for parallel, isolated implementation or large code-generation/refactor work that should run off the principal CC thread. Routes to OpenAI Codex (gpt-5.x) via `codex exec` inside a sandboxed worktree. Opt-in only (`@codex` or `mooter spawn`) until the pricing gate opens — never auto-selected. ([pauloloureiroshp-ship-it/mooter](https://github.com/pauloloureiroshp-ship-it/mooter/blob/HEAD/_handoff/codex/scaffold/agents/codex-specialist.md))
